## Group 161 (starts row 800)

### Row 800

- **A800** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B800** (New rule): When correcting or updating a record, edit or delete the entry (including a wrong learning) rather than adding a contradicting one, and delete abandoned mostly-wrong documents. The one exception: a record kept as history or someone's own account of a problem (e.g. the owner's problem description, the rule inbox) is never edited or deleted; correct it by appending an entry that names what it supersedes, and keep duplicates with a note.
- **C800** (Existing rule): M142: - The inbox is append-only history. Entries are never deleted, and a duplicate is kept with a note rather than tidied away.
- **D800** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E800** (Kind): RULE-PROCESS
- **F800** (Test type): _empty_
- **G800** (Plugin): machinery
- **H800** (Location): plugins/machinery/rules/rule-governance.md § Dictating a rule
- **I800** (Trigger): editing the inbox

### Row 801 (new rule in B800)

- **A801** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B801** (New rule): _empty_
- **C801** (Existing rule): M270: - Never rewrite the owner's own description of a problem. It is their record of what they saw, and editing it destroys the only account of the symptom that is not yours.
- **D801** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E801** (Kind): RECORDS
- **F801** (Test type): _empty_
- **G801** (Plugin): machinery
- **H801** (Location): plugins/machinery/rules/work-tracking.md § The owner's own list
- **I801** (Trigger): owner's problem description exists

### Row 802 (new rule in B800)

- **A802** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B802** (New rule): _empty_
- **C802** (Existing rule): D33: - **Prefer editing an entry to adding one.** Records grow by accretion unless something opposes it. Deleting a stale entry is maintenance, not loss. **One exception, and it is the only one:** a record kept as history, and someone's own account of a problem, are added to and never edited or deleted — neither an entry within one, nor the record itself. What makes a record one of these is what it holds, not what it is called or where it lives. Their value is that they say what was true, or what someone saw, at the time they were written; editing one destroys the only copy of that, and deleting it destroys every copy at once, leaving nothing that could disagree with today's story. Correct them by appending, saying what the new entry supersedes. Every other record — anything holding neither of those two things — follows the edit-or-delete rule above.
- **D802** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E802** (Kind): RECORDS
- **F802** (Test type): _empty_
- **G802** (Plugin): developer-friendliness
- **H802** (Location): claude-code/developer-friendliness/developer-friendliness/SKILL.md § 5.1 Writing
- **I802** (Trigger): updating a record

### Row 803 (new rule in B800)

- **A803** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B803** (New rule): _empty_
- **C803** (Existing rule): D54: | A learning that was wrong | fix or delete it; never append a correction beside it — unless it lives in one of §5.1's two append-only records, where the correction is appended and names what it supersedes | two contradicting notes are worse than neither — the reader must adjudicate with less than you had, and an appended correction that does not say what it replaces leaves them exactly there |
- **D803** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E803** (Kind): RECORDS
- **F803** (Test type): _empty_
- **G803** (Plugin): developer-friendliness
- **H803** (Location): claude-code/developer-friendliness/developer-friendliness/SKILL.md § 6.2 Learnings | A learning that was wrong
- **I803** (Trigger): a learning proved wrong

### Row 804 (new rule in B800)

- **A804** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B804** (New rule): _empty_
- **C804** (Existing rule): D93: - **Deleting is repair, with one exception.** An abandoned document that is eighty percent wrong is worse than no document, because it is believed by whoever has not yet been burned by it. The exception is §5.1's: a record kept as history, and someone's own account of a problem, are not yours to delete or rewrite however wrong they read — and deleting the record outright is the plainest case of that, not a way around it. Being wrong is part of what they record, and the person who wrote one is the only one who can say it no longer holds. Every other abandoned document is deletable, which is what makes deleting repair.
- **D804** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E804** (Kind): RECORDS
- **F804** (Test type): _empty_
- **G804** (Plugin): developer-friendliness
- **H804** (Location): claude-code/developer-friendliness/developer-friendliness/SKILL.md § 8. Arriving at a record that is already bad
- **I804** (Trigger): abandoned mostly-wrong document

## Group 162 (starts row 805)

### Row 805

- **A805** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B805** (New rule): When you inherit a stale or split record, do not start a new place: correct the entries you prove wrong in records the current work relies on, file (not fix) stale entries elsewhere, and let trust recover cumulatively rather than through an unrequested audit.
- **C805** (Existing rule): D79: | A stale entry noticed in passing | corrected, if it is in a record the current work reads or relies on; anywhere else, filed and left alone | leaving it where you are working costs every future reader doubt about everything near it, and you are the one person already holding what makes it wrong; going after one outside that spends this session on somebody else's record |
- **D805** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E805** (Kind): RECORDS
- **F805** (Test type): _empty_
- **G805** (Plugin): developer-friendliness
- **H805** (Location): claude-code/developer-friendliness/developer-friendliness/SKILL.md § 6.7 Keeping the record true | A stale entry noticed in passing
- **I805** (Trigger): stale entry noticed in passing

## Group 163 (starts row 809)

- **A809** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B809** (New rule): If there is genuinely nowhere to put a record, ask the developer once and apply the answer everywhere; never invent a convention for them.
- **C809** (Existing rule): D27: If there is genuinely no place for something, that is worth one question to the developer — asked once, applied everywhere afterward, and never converted into a new convention you invented on their behalf.
- **D809** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E809** (Kind): COMMUNICATION
- **F809** (Test type): _empty_
- **G809** (Plugin): developer-friendliness
- **H809** (Location): claude-code/developer-friendliness/developer-friendliness/SKILL.md § 3.2 Where will they look for it?
- **I809** (Trigger): no place exists for a record

- **A810** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B810** (New rule): _empty_
- **C810** (Existing rule): D94: - **If there is genuinely nowhere to put things**, that is one question to the developer, and it is worth asking, because every later choice depends on the answer. Ask it once and apply the answer everywhere.

- **E810** (Kind): COMMUNICATION

## Group 164 (starts row 811)

### Row 811

- **A811** (Group plugin): developer-friendliness
- **B811** (New rule): Write a report (read once, now, with full context) and a record (read later, cold) as separate artifacts.
- **C811** (Existing rule): D34: - **The report and the record are two masters.** A message is read once, now, by someone with full context; a record is read later, cold, by someone with none. One artifact serving both is either unreadable or assumes context its reader lacks.
- **D811** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E811** (Kind): RECORDS
- **F811** (Test type): _empty_
- **G811** (Plugin): developer-friendliness
- **H811** (Location): claude-code/developer-friendliness/developer-friendliness/SKILL.md § 5.1 Writing
- **I811** (Trigger): writing both report and record

## Group 165 (starts row 812)

### Row 812

- **A812** (Group plugin): machinery
- **B812** (New rule): When the owner corrects something, update both halves of the pair. When a pair is blocked on the owner's decision, label both halves blocked and comment on the companion exactly what input is needed; unattended passes skip it.

## Group 168 (starts row 818)

### Row 818

- **A818** (Group plugin): machinery
- **B818** (New rule): A project adds tools the universal catalog lacks to .claude/machinery/tool-catalog.json; when a project entry holds everywhere, promote it with promote-tool.mjs, which refuses unless the project fixture proves the answer line survives filtering and bumps the plugin version (bump by hand if that fails). Never overwrite an existing universal entry without --force, and give each universal entry a verified note naming the tool version measured and what was observed. Try the quiet flag the filter suggests for a noisy catalogued tool.
- **C818** (Existing rule): M482: The universal tool catalog (`data/tool-catalog.json`) names each off-the-shelf tool, the line that is its answer, and its documented quiet flags; a project adds its own entries to `.claude/machinery/tool-catalog.json`.
- **D818** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E818** (Kind): TOOLING
- **F818** (Test type): _empty_
- **G818** (Plugin): machinery
- **H818** (Location): plugins/machinery/README.md § Teaching it a tool
- **I818** (Trigger): project-specific tool entry

### Row 819 (new rule in B818)

- **A819** (Group plugin): machinery
- **B819** (New rule): _empty_
- **C819** (Existing rule): M483: When a project entry turns out to be true everywhere, promote it with `node "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/promote-tool.mjs" --id <id> --root <project root>` — it refuses unless the project's fixture proves the outcome line survives filtering, then moves the entry into the universal catalog and bumps the plugin version.
- **D819** (SDLC stage): ANY
- **E819** (Kind): TOOLING
- **F819** (Test type): _empty_
- **G819** (Plugin): machinery
- **H819** (Location): plugins/machinery/README.md § Teaching it a tool
- **I819** (Trigger): project tool entry true everywhere
